Parse the XML\n    tree = ET.parse(section_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified = False\n\n    # Process each paragraph\n    for para in root.findall('.//hp:p', NSMAP):\n        # Collect all text from hp:t elements within this paragraph\n        t_elements = para.findall('.//hp:t', NSMAP)\n\n        if not t_elements:\n            continue\n\n        # Join all text to reconstruct the full paragraph text\n        full_text = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in t_elements)\n\n        # Perform replacements\n        new_text = full_text\n        for placeholder, value in replacements.items():\n            new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n        # If text was modified, update and remove lineSegArray\n        if new_text != full_text:\n            modified = True\n\n            # Remove all hp:lineSegArray elements from this paragraph\n            for line_seg in para.findall('hp:lineSegArray', NSMAP):\n                para.remove(line_seg)\n\n            # Put all replaced text into the first hp:t element\n            if t_elements:\n                t_elements[0].text = new_text\n                # Clear the rest\n                for t in t_elements[1:]:\n                    t.text = ''\n\n    # Write back\n    tree.write(section_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return modified\n\ndef main():\n    # Extract the template\n    work_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work'\n    extracted_dir = os.path.join(work_dir, 'template_extracted')\n\n    # Build replacements dict from overview data\n    summary = overview['summary']\n    risk_tier = summary['위험등급']\n    severity_note = RISK_SEVERITY_MAP.get(risk_tier, '')\n    risk_with_severity = f\"{risk_tier} ({severity_note})\"\n\n    # Date reformatting: YYYY-MM-DD -> YYYY.MM.DD\n    inspection_date = summary['점검일']\n    formatted_date = re.sub(r'(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})', r'\\1.\\2.\\3', inspection_date)\n\n    # Get corrective actions\n    action_list = actions['immediate_actions']\n\n    # Build replacements for section0\n    replacements_s0 = {\n        '{{점검ID}}': summary['점검ID'],\n        '{{현장명}}': summary['현장명'],\n        '{{주관점검자}}': summary['주관점검자'],\n        '{{점검일}}': formatted_date,\n        '{{위험등급}}': risk_with_severity,\n        '{{총평}}': summary['총평'],\n    }\n\n    # Build replacements for section1\n    replacements_s1 = {\n        '{{조치1}}': action_list[0] if len(action_list) > 0 else '',\n        '{{조치2}}': action_list[1] if len(action_list) > 1 else '',\n        '{{조치3}}': action_list[2] if len(action_list) > 2 else '',\n        '{{보고대상}}': summary['보고대상'],\n    }\n\n    # Process section0.xml\n    section0_path = os.path.join(extracted_dir, 'Contents', 'section0.xml')\n    process_section(section0_path, replacements_s0)\n\n    # Process section1.xml\n    section1_path = os.path.join(extracted_dir, 'Contents', 'section1.xml')\n    process_section(section1_path, replacements_s1)\n\n    # Repackage the HWPX\n    output_path = '/root/safety_audit_brief_final.hwpx'\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root_dir, dirs, files in os.walk(extracted_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                file_path = os.path.join(root_dir, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, extracted_dir)\n                zf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n    print(f\"Output written to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(\"\\n--- Validation ---\")\n\n    # Re-extract and check\n    verify_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_verify'\n    os.makedirs(verify_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'r') as zf:\n        zf.extractall(verify_dir)\n\n    # Check for remaining placeholders\n    all_content = content0 + content1\n    remaining_placeholders = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', all_content)\n\n    if remaining_placeholders:\n        print(f\"FAIL: Placeholders remain: {remaining_placeholders}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"PASS: No placeholders remain\")\n\n    # Check date format\n    old_date_format = re.findall(r'\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}', all_content)\n    if old_date_format:\n        print(f\"FAIL: Old date format found: {old_date_format}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"PASS: Dates are in YYYY.MM.DD format\")\n\n    #
